10 Functions of regional councils
For the purposes of this schedule, a regional council has the following functions:
to enforce the observance of the requirements of this schedule and regulations to the extent that its powers under this Act enable it to do so; and
to monitor compliance by farm operators with their duties under this schedule and with any requirements in regulations; and
to receive notifications of freshwater farm plans that have been certified in whole or in part; and
to appoint certifiers and auditors under clause 12; and
to receive audit reports and related notifications from auditors; and
to monitor the delivery of certification or audit services, or both, by approved industry organisations in the council’s region for compliance with this schedule and with any applicable requirements prescribed in regulations.
A regional council may do all or any of the following:
require a farm operator to produce a freshwater farm plan for inspection:
require information from an approved industry organisation that the council considers reasonably necessary for carrying out its functions under this clause:
notify the Minister of any significant or persistent concerns regarding the performance under this schedule of an approved industry organisation operating in the council’s region, including concerns arising in the course of the council’s exercise of its functions under subclause (1)﻿(f).
11 Records that must be kept by regional council
A regional council must keep and maintain, in relation to each farm in its jurisdiction, a record of—
whether the farm’s freshwater farm plan or a part of a freshwater farm plan is required to be certified; and
if the plan or part of a plan is certified, the date on which it was certified; and
in the case of a limited certified freshwater farm plan, the prescribed activity or activities to which certification relates; and
if the farm is required under clause 6(2)﻿(c) to be audited, the date on which the farm was last audited for compliance with the freshwater farm plan; and
any other information required by regulations.
12 Regional council must appoint certifiers and auditors
A regional council must—
appoint 1 or more certifiers; and
appoint 1 or more auditors.
A regional council may make an appointment under this clause only if satisfied that criteria prescribed in regulations have been met.
13 Minister may approve industry organisation to provide certification or audit services
The Minister may, on application, approve an industry organisation to provide certification or audit services, or both, under this schedule to its members—
if the Minister is satisfied that the organisation meets any applicable eligibility requirements prescribed in regulations; and
after consulting regional councils and the Minister of Agriculture.
An approved industry organisation may appoint certifiers or auditors if it is satisfied that the applicable requirements have been met as prescribed in regulations.
The Minister may revoke an industry organisation’s approval—
if the Minister is satisfied that the applicable requirements for revocation have been met, as prescribed in regulations; and
after consulting regional councils, the Minister of Agriculture, and the industry organisation.
An approval or a revocation of approval of an industry organisation may apply either nationally or in respect of 1 or more regions.
Any appointment of certifiers or auditors that was made by an approved industry organisation under subclause (2) ceases to the extent that the industry organisation’s approval is revoked under subclause (3).
The Minister may request from an industry organisation information that the Minister considers reasonably necessary before deciding to approve the organisation or revoke its approval.
14 Relationship between freshwater farm plan and specified instruments
A freshwater farm plan may contain a requirement that—
relates to an activity carried out on the farm (an activity) even if there is no similar requirement relating to that activity in a provision of a specified instrument; or
restricts an activity more than a provision of a specified instrument.
However, if a provision of a specified instrument restricts an activity more than a requirement of a freshwater farm plan, the provision of the specified instrument prevails.
To avoid doubt, compliance with a requirement of a certified freshwater farm plan or a limited certified freshwater farm plan—
does not of itself authorise a person to undertake an activity:
may be specified or included as a requirement or condition in any specified instrument relating to an activity.
15 Regulations relating to freshwater farm plans
The Governor-General may, by Order in Council made on the recommendation of the Minister after consulting the Minister of Agriculture, make regulations that—
prescribe crops for the purposes of the definition of arable land use in clause 2:
prescribe agricultural land uses for the purposes of the definition of farm in clause 2:
prescribe the area of land described in clause 5(1)﻿(d) (in relation to agricultural land use prescribed under paragraph (b)):
prescribe the kinds of farm activities or catchments in respect of which a certified freshwater farm plan or a limited certified freshwater farm plan is required:
provide for the content of a freshwater farm plan, including (without limitation) specifying—
any requirements, including actions, criteria, methods, or thresholds for the purpose of identifying, measuring, avoiding, minimising, remedying, or of offsetting or compensating when appropriate and enabled in a plan, adverse effects of activities carried out on the farm on freshwater and freshwater ecosystems; and
outcomes that must be achieved for the purpose of avoiding, minimising, remedying, or of offsetting or compensating when appropriate and enabled in a plan, those adverse effects on freshwater and freshwater ecosystems; and
any requirements, including actions, criteria, methods, thresholds, or outcomes, for the purpose of achieving relevant human health or environmental limits; and
any other information that must be included in the plan for the purpose of this schedule:
provide for the form and manner in which a freshwater farm plan or part of a plan that must be certified is certified, including (without limitation) prescribing—
time frames that must be complied with by the farm operator and certifier; and
any fees payable by the farm operator or the manner of calculating those fees:
prescribe the circumstances in which a certified freshwater farm plan or a limited certified freshwater farm plan must be amended and recertified:
prescribe requirements that must be met for the approval of industry organisations under clause 13 or the revocation of their approval, including any further obligations for approved industry organisations:
provide for the form and manner in which a farm or part of a farm that must be audited for compliance with a freshwater farm plan or part of a freshwater farm plan is audited, including (without limitation) prescribing—
the time frame within which a farm must be audited; and
the frequency with which those audits must be carried out; and
the period after which the auditor must provide their final report; and
any matters that an auditor must take into account when considering whether the farm achieves compliance with the freshwater farm plan; and
the information that the farm operator must provide to the auditor for the purpose of the audit; and
any fees payable by the farm operator or the manner of calculating those fees:
prescribe criteria that apply to the appointment of a person as an auditor or certifier and their continuation in that role:
require auditors, certifiers, and farm operators to supply prescribed information to regional councils for the purpose of clause 10:
prescribe information that a regional council must keep in relation to farms in its jurisdiction:
prescribe infringement offences for the contravention of, or non-compliance with, a provision of this schedule or of any regulations made under this clause:
provide for any other matters that are contemplated by, or necessary for giving full effect to, this schedule and for its due administration.
Regulations under this clause may apply generally or to specified districts, regions, or parts of New Zealand.
Regulations under this clause may apply to all certifiers or auditors generally, or may apply only to certifiers and auditors appointed—
by a regional council under clause 12(1); or
by an approved industry organisation under clause 13(2).
Regulations under this clause may incorporate material by reference under section 91 (which applies as if references in that section to national standards were references to regulations under this clause).
Regulations under this clause are secondary legislation (see Part 3 of the Legislation Act 2019 for publication requirements).
